Appendix E
Auxiliary Expectation Derivations

In this section, we attach many auxiliary derivations of simple and complicated polynomials that we need in our main propositions and lemmas.

Proposition 14
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{3}\right]=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m^{2}-2mp-3m+p^{2}+6p+14\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

Proof.

We notice that the expectation can be written as

\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{3}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left({{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}+{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}}\right)^{3}\right]\,.

Employing the algebraic identity that
\left({a+b}\right)^{3}=a^{3}+3a^{2}b+3ab^{2}+b^{3}

and Cor.
12
(canceling terms with an odd number of
\mathbf{Q}_{m}
appearances),
it can be readily seen that in our case we are left with
\left({a+b}\right)^{3}=\cancel{a^{3}}+{3a^{2}b}+\cancel{3ab^{2}}+b^{3}
.
We thus get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{3}\right]=3\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=3\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}u_{i}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}u_{i}^{2}\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=3\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{2}u_{i}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}\sum_{j=m+1}^{p}\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}^{2}u_{j}^{2}u_{k}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(3\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right]+\sum_{j=m+1}^{p}\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}u_{j}^{2}u_{k}^{2}\right]\right)

The inner left term becomes

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{2}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right]=\frac{3}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{4}\right]=\frac{3}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}u_{i}^{2}u_{j}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{m}\left(\underbrace{m\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}u_{1}^{4}\right]}_{i=j}+\underbrace{m\left(m-1\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}u_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{2}\right]}_{i\neq j}\right)=3\left(\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2\\
2\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\right)\,,

while the inner right term becomes

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{4}u_{k}^{2}\right]}_{j=p}+\underbrace{\left(p-m-1\right)\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p-1}^{2}u_{p}^{2}u_{k}^{2}\right]}_{m+1\leq j\leq p-1}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{6}\right]}_{k=p}+\underbrace{\left(p\!-\!m\!-\!1\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p-1}^{2}u_{p}^{4}\right]}_{m+1\leq k\leq p-1}+\left(p\!-\!m\!-\!1\right)\left(\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p-1}^{2}u_{p}^{4}\right]}_{k=p\,\vee\,k=p-1}+\underbrace{\left(p\!-\!m\!-\!2\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p-2}^{2}u_{p-1}^{2}u_{p}^{2}\right]}_{m+1\leq j\leq p-2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}6\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(p-m-1\right)\left(3\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(p-m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2\\
2\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\right)\,.

Combining these two inner terms, we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}6\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+3\left(p-m\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m^{2}-2mp+6m+p^{2}-3p-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2\\
2\\
2\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(\frac{15}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\frac{9\left(p-m\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\frac{m^{2}-2mp+6m+p^{2}-3p-1}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m^{2}-2mp-3m+p^{2}+6p+14\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 15
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right]=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+2mp+3m-6\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

Proof.

By decomposing the expectation into the two additive terms below,
we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}+{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}+{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\right]}_{\text{below}}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\right]}_{\text{below}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m\left(p+2\right)-4-m^{2}+mp+m-2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+2mp+3m-6\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

Using Cor.
12
in the first step below, we show that the first term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right]+\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{b}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:ua2(ubvb)2})}}+\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{u}}_{b}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:ua2va2ub2})}}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{m}\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{1}{m}\tfrac{m\left(p-m\right)\left(m\left(p+3\right)-2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m\left(p+2\right)-4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

From Eq. (
10
),
we know that the second term is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uava)2})}}-\left(1+\frac{1}{m}\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:ua2(ubvb)2})}}=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)}-\tfrac{m+1}{m}\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+m-2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 16
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{4}=\frac{3\left(m+4\right)\left(m+6\right)+\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m^{2}-2mp-4m+p^{2}+10p+36\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

Proof.

We start by showing,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]{\mathbf{u}}\right)^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}\right)^{4}\,.

Employing an algebraic identity and Cor.
12
to cancel terms with an odd number of
\mathbf{Q}_{m}
appearances,
it can be readily seen that in our case we are left with

\left({a+b}\right)^{4}=a^{4}+\cancel{4a^{3}b}+6a^{2}b^{2}+\cancel{4ab^{3}}+b^{4}
.
We thus get,

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{4}+6\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1},\mathbf{u}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right)^{4}+6\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left(\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right)^{4}\right]+6\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

We employ the isotropy of
\mathbf{r}
and show that,

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}^{\top}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{4}\right]+\frac{6}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[r_{1}^{4}\right]\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}+\frac{6}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle_{m}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}+\frac{6}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\right)^{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{m\left(m+2\right)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}+\frac{6}{m}\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}-2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{3}{m\left(m+2\right)}+\frac{6}{m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}+\frac{6}{m}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}-\frac{12}{m}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{6}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}

\displaystyle=\frac{6m+15}{m\left(m+2\right)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{8}+\frac{6}{m}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}-\frac{12}{m}\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{6}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{8}\,,

where we use the subscript in
\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4\\
\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle_{m}

to indicate that, unlike in most places, the corresponding random vector is in
\mathcal{S}^{m-1}
rather than
\mathcal{S}^{p-1}
.

Next, we derive these expected norms in Prop.
26
,
and obtain

\displaystyle=\frac{6m+15}{m\left(m+2\right)}\frac{m\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(m+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\frac{6}{m}\frac{m\left(m+2\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)}-\frac{12}{m}\frac{m\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(p-m+4\right)\left(p-m+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(6m+15\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(m+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\frac{6\left(m+2\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)}-\frac{12\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(p-m+4\right)\left(p-m+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(6m+15\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(m+6\right)+6\left(m+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)-12\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(p-m+4\right)\left(p-m+6\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\left(m+4\right)\left(m+6\right)+\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m^{2}-2mp-4m+p^{2}+10p+36\right)}{p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 17
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right)^{4}=\tfrac{3\left(m^{4}-2m^{3}\left(2p+3\right)+m^{2}\left(4p^{2}+4p-1\right)+2m\left(6p^{2}+8p+3\right)+8\left(p^{2}-2p-3\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

We begin by showing that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right)^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{p}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{Q}_{p}^{\top}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right)^{4}=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left(\mathbf{u}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}\right)^{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{4}\,,

where the last step simply employs our simplifying notations
(Remark
4
).

Employing an algebraic identity and Cor.
12
to cancel terms with an odd number of
\mathbf{Q}_{m}
appearances,
it can be readily seen that in our case we are left with

\left({a+b}\right)^{4}=a^{4}+\cancel{4a^{3}b}+6a^{2}b^{2}+\cancel{4ab^{3}}+b^{4}
.
We thus get,

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{4}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmva)4})}}+6\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmva)2(uv)2})}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{4}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uava)4})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{3\left(m^{2}\left(p+3\right)\left(p+5\right)+2m\left(p+1\right)\left(p+3\right)-8\left(2p+3\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+6\tfrac{\left(m+2\right)\left(mp+2p+3m\right)\left(p-m\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{3m^{4}-6m^{3}p+3m^{2}p^{2}-6m^{2}p-12m^{2}+6mp^{2}+12mp}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

After some tedious algebra,
we get, as required,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right)^{4}=\tfrac{3\left(m^{4}-2m^{3}\left(2p+3\right)+m^{2}\left(4p^{2}+4p-1\right)+2m\left(6p^{2}+8p+3\right)+8\left(p^{2}-2p-3\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 18
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(m+4\right)\left(2mp+4p+m-m^{2}-6\right)-\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m\left(m-2p-5\right)+10\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

First, due to the exchangeability (Prop.
9
), we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}\!=\!\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\right)^{2}\!=\!\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}^{\top}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}^{\top}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}\!=\!\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}

Then, we show that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left(\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]+\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\right){\mathbf{u}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left(\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]+\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\right){\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}+2{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}+\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{b}}\right\|^{4}\right)\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2}

We now partition the above into three terms that we solve separately.

The first term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}\right)^{2}+2{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}+\left({\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}\right)^{2}}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmua)2(uaQmva)2})}}+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}}_{=0\text{, by Prop.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2}}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmua)2(ubvb)2})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(m+4\right)\left(m\left(p+3\right)+2\left(p-3\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\frac{\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(p-m\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(m+4\right)\left(2mp+4p+m-m^{2}-6\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

The second term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}+2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Prop.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]\cdot\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{r}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)\right]\cdot\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]\mathbf{v}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]=\frac{4}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(1-\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(-\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)4(ubvb)2})}}-\frac{4}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:ua2(ubvb)2})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{m}\frac{m\left(m+2\right)\left(m+4\right)\left(p-m\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}-\frac{4}{m}\frac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(\left(m+4\right)-\left(p+6\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}=\frac{-4\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2p+4-m^{2}\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

The third term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left(\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}+2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{=0\text{, by Prop.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)4(uaQmva)2})}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{4}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)4(ubvb)2})}\\
\text{(plugging in $m\leftarrow p-m$})\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(mp^{2}-m^{2}p-5m^{2}+7mp+12m-2p-4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m^{2}-2mp-6m+p^{2}+6p+8\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m^{3}-3m^{2}p-11m^{2}+2mp^{2}+13mp+20m-2p-4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

Finally, summing the three terms (and after some tedious algebra), we get the required

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{2}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}=\tfrac{\left(m+4\right)\left(2mp+4p+m-m^{2}-6\right)-\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m\left(m-2p-5\right)+10\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 19
.

For
p\geq 3,m\in\{2,3,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{3}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}=\tfrac{4mp\left(-m^{2}+m+4\right)+4\left(m+1\right)\left(m+2\right)p^{2}+\left(m-6\right)\left(m-1\right)m\left(m+1\right)-8\left(2p+3\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

We show that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{3}\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}{\mathbf{e}}_{1}\right)^{2}\!

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left(\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]+\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\right){\mathbf{z}}\cdot{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}\left(\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right]+\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{0}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\right){\mathbf{v}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{z}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{b}\right)^{2}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{z}}_{a}\right)^{2}+2{\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{z}}_{a}{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{b}+\left({\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{z}}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\left({\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}{\mathbf{v}}_{a}+{\mathbf{u}}_{b}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{b}\right)^{2},

where the expectation is computed over the orthogonal matrix
\mathbf{Q}_{m}\sim O(m)
and three random (isotropic) orthogonal unit vectors
\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}\perp\mathbf{z}\in\mathcal{S}^{p-1}
.

Again, starting from the first additive term,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}+2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmza)^2(uaQmva)^2})}}+\underbrace{2\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}}_{=0\text{, by Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmza)^2(ubvb)^2})}}\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{m\left(p+3\right)\left(\left(m+2\right)p+5m+2\right)-16p-24}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(m\left(p^{2}+5p+2\right)-6p-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-2\right)\left(m\left(p+3\right)\left(\left(m+2\right)p+5m+2\right)-16p-24\right)+\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(m\left(p^{2}+5p+2\right)-6p-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

The second term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\left(\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}+2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{=0,\text{ by Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}}+4\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{=0,\text{ by Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}}

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}\perp\mathbf{z},\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}\perp\mathbf{z}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\!\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]=\frac{4}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}\perp\mathbf{z}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)^2vazaubzbubvb})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\left(p-m\right)\left(\left(m+2\right)p^{2}+\left(2-3m\right)p-2m^{2}\left(p+2\right)-6m+4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

And the third additive term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)^{2}\left(\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}+2\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\begin{subarray}{c}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]\\
\text{due to the invariance (Prop.~\ref {prop:invariance}),}\\
\text{already solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmza)^2(ubvb)^2})}\end{subarray}}+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{=0\text{, by Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ubzb)^2(ubvb)^2})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(m\left(p^{2}+5p+2\right)-6p-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{m\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+2p+4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(\left(m+2\right)\left(m\left(p^{2}+5p+2\right)-6p-4\right)+\left(p-2\right)m\left(-m^{2}+mp+2p+4\right)\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

By adding these three terms and after some tedious algebra, we get the required proposition.
∎

Proposition 20
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right]=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp-m+p-2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

Proof.

First, we notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v},\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\!\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\!\left[\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|\mathbf{r}\right)^{\top}\right]\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\!\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\!\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]=-\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right].

Then, we focus on our quantity of interest here,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v},\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\left[\mathbf{v}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]\,.

Splitting the first multiplicative term, we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

Cor.
12

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(vaQmua)(uaQmva)})}}\,,

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{above}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,,

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]\\
\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}\end{subarray}\right]&=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{b}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]\\
&\hskip 10.00002pt\left[\text{invariance of $\mathbf{Q}_{m}$ w.r.t. transpose (Prop.~\ref {prop:invariance})}\right]\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]\\
\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{above}\end{subarray}\right]&=-\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{u}\perp\mathbf{v}}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]\,.\end{split}

(10)

Combining the above, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uava)2})}}-\left(1+\frac{2}{m}\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:ua2(ubvb)2})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)}-\tfrac{m+2}{m}\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m+2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp-m+p-2\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 21
.

For
p\geq 3,m\in\{2,3,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right]=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(2m^{2}-3mp-2m-p+8\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

Proof.

We start by decomposing the expectation into the following,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{z}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{u}\cdot\mathbf{z}^{\top}\left[\begin{smallmatrix}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\\
&\mathbf{I}_{p-m}\end{smallmatrix}\right]\mathbf{v}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\cdot\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\cdot\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\cdot\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

Focusing on the first additive term and by employing Cor.
12
(in the first step below), we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\left(\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}v_{i}q_{ij}u_{j}\right)\left(\sum_{k,\ell=1}^{m}z_{k}q_{k\ell}v_{\ell}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\underbrace{\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}}_{=-\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{s=m+1}^{p}u_{s}z_{s}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}v_{i}q_{ij}u_{j}\right)\left(\sum_{k,\ell=1}^{m}z_{k}q_{k\ell}v_{\ell}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)^2(vbzb)^2})}}+\left(p-m\right)\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\sum_{k,\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{j}u_{p}v_{i}v_{\ell}z_{k}z_{p}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}q_{k\ell}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\left[\text{By Prop.~\ref {prop:odd}, most summands are zero}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\frac{m\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\left(p-m\right)\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{j}u_{p}v_{i}v_{j}z_{i}z_{p}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\frac{p-m}{m}\left(\underbrace{m\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}u_{p}v_{1}^{2}z_{1}z_{p}\right]}_{i=j}+\underbrace{m\left(m-1\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{2}u_{p}v_{1}v_{2}z_{1}z_{p}\right]}_{i\neq j}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\left(p-m\right)\left(\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}1&2&1\\
1&0&1\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}0&1&1\\
1&1&0\\
1&0&1\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(-\tfrac{mp+2m-4}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{-1}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{2\left(m-1\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(\frac{-mp+2}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\frac{-1}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-mp-p+4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

In addition, the second term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{z}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left[\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\right]\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left\|\mathbf{z}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\underbrace{\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}}_{=-\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}}\bigg]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\underbrace{\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}}_{=-\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{z}_{a}\right\|^{2}\left(\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)^2(vbzb)^2})}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{m}\frac{m\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}-\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}v_{i}u_{k}z_{k}v_{\ell}z_{\ell}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}-m\sum_{i=m+1}^{p}\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[v_{1}z_{1}u_{i}v_{i}u_{k}z_{k}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(mp+2m-4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}-\left(p-m\right)m\sum_{k=m+1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[v_{1}z_{1}u_{p}v_{p}u_{k}z_{k}\right]

\displaystyle=-\left(p-m\right)\Bigg(\tfrac{mp+2m-4}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+m\left(\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p}^{2}v_{1}v_{p}z_{1}z_{p}\right]}_{k=p}+\underbrace{\left(p-m-1\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{p-1}u_{p}v_{1}v_{p-1}z_{1}z_{p}\right]}_{m+1\leq k\leq p-1}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=-\left(p-m\right)\left(\tfrac{mp+2m-4}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+m\left(\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}0&1&1\\
2&1&1\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(p-m-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}0&1&1\\
1&1&0\\
1&0&1\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-\left(p-m\right)\left(\tfrac{mp+2m-4}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+m\left(\tfrac{-1}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{2\left(p-m-1\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{-2\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+m-2\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

Overall, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-mp-p+4\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}+\tfrac{-2\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+m-2\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(2m^{2}-3mp-2m-p+8\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 22
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{4}\left(p+3\right)+m^{3}\left(3p^{2}+8p-6\right)+m^{2}\left(-3p^{3}-6p^{2}+13p+3\right)+m\left(p^{4}-7p^{2}+4p+6\right)+p^{4}-p^{3}-18p^{2}-40p-24}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

We start by decomposing the quantity as follows,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

We show that the first term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{equivalent}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left(\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Prop.~\ref {prop:(uaQmua)(uaQmva)(vaQmua)(vaQmVa)}}}+\tfrac{2}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{b}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)^2(uava)(ubvb)(vb)^2})}}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)^{2}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmua)(vaQmva)(vbub)^2})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-\left(m^{2}\left(2p+3\right)+m\left(-p^{2}+14p+30\right)-6p^{2}+4p+24\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{2m\left(p-m\right)\left(p+3\right)\left(m^{2}-mp-2\left(p+2\right)\right)}{m\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{3\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+2p+4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{3}\left(2p+3\right)+m^{2}\left(4p^{2}+4p-3\right)-2m\left(p^{3}-p^{2}+9\right)-4\left(p^{3}+2p^{2}+4p+6\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Moreover, we show that the second term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right)^{2}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{equal due to the invariance of $\mathbf{Q}_{m}$ w.r.t. transpose (Prop.~\ref {prop:invariance})}}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right)^{2}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(vb)^2(vaua)^2})}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(vb)^2(uaQmva)(vaQmua)})}}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(ubvb)(uaQmva)(vaQmva)})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)m\left(m^{2}\left(p+3\right)-2m\left(p^{2}+5p+3\right)+p\left(p^{2}+7p+10\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m^{2}p+3m^{2}-2mp^{2}-10mp-6m+p^{3}+7p^{2}+10p\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{-2\left(p-m\right)\left(p+3\right)\left(3\left(p+1\right)+\left(m-1\right)\left(p-m-1\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m^{2}p+3m^{2}-mp^{2}-2mp+9m-p^{2}-p+12\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Overall, we conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{3}\left(2p+3\right)+m^{2}\left(4p^{2}+4p-3\right)-2m\left(p^{3}-p^{2}+9\right)-4\left(p^{3}+2p^{2}+4p+6\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{-\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(m^{2}p+3m^{2}-mp^{2}-2mp+9m-p^{2}-p+12\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{4}\left(p+3\right)+m^{3}\left(3p^{2}+8p-6\right)+m^{2}\left(-3p^{3}-6p^{2}+13p+3\right)+m\left(p^{4}-7p^{2}+4p+6\right)+p^{4}-p^{3}-18p^{2}-40p-24}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 23
.

For
p\geq 2,m\in\{2,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]=\tfrac{-\left(m^{2}\left(2p+3\right)+m\left(-p^{2}+14p+30\right)-6p^{2}+4p+24\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{v}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\sum_{k,\ell=1}^{m}\sum_{n,r=1}^{m}\sum_{s,t=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}u_{k}v_{\ell}v_{n}u_{r}v_{s}v_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}q_{k\ell}q_{nr}q_{st}\right]

∎
