Proposition 24
.

For
p\geq 3,m\in\{2,3,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{2m^{4}p+4m^{4}-7m^{3}p^{2}-18m^{3}p+8m^{2}p^{3}+25m^{2}p^{2}+24m^{2}p}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{20m^{2}-3mp^{4}-11mp^{3}-44mp^{2}-64mp-24m-p^{4}+11p^{3}+32p^{2}+68p+48}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

Proof.

Like in previous proofs, we decompose the expression into,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

(11)

Focusing on the first term, and employing Cor.
12
,
we see that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

The polynomial in the first summand can be derived tediously, very much like in the proof of the former Prop.
23
,
and shown to hold

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]=\tfrac{-m^{2}\left(2p^{2}+9p+6\right)+m\left(p-2\right)\left(p^{2}-6\right)+2p^{3}+32p+48}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

The sum of the three rightmost summands is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}q_{ij}u_{k}v_{\ell}q_{k\ell}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}q_{ij}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}z_{k}u_{\ell}q_{k\ell}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}q_{ij}z_{k}v_{\ell}q_{k\ell}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}q_{k\ell}\right]\left(\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}u_{k}v_{\ell}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}z_{k}u_{\ell}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}q_{k\ell}\right]\left(\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}z_{k}v_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[q_{ij}^{2}\right]}_{=1/m}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}u_{i}v_{j}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}z_{i}u_{j}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}u_{j}z_{i}v_{j}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\left(\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\!\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\Bigg(\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{obtained from Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ua)^2vazaubzbubvb}) by plugging in $m\leftarrow p\!-\!m$}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right]}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ubzb)^2(ubvb)^2})}\\
\text{since $\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}=-\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}$
and
$\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{a}=-\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}$
}\end{subarray}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=2\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(2\left(p-m\right)p+4\left(p-m\right)-p^{2}-3p+2\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-m^{2}+mp+2p+4\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\left(p-m\right)\left(m+2\right)\left(5mp+6m-3p^{2}-2p\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Overall, the first term of Eq. (
11
) equals,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{6\left(m^{3}+m^{2}+2m+8\right)+4\left(m+2\right)p^{3}-2\left(5m^{2}+8m-2\right)p^{2}+\left(m-2\right)\left(5m^{2}+3m-16\right)p}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

The second term holds,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{equal (swap $\mathbf{v},\mathbf{z}$ and map $\mathbf{Q}\to\mathbf{Q}^{\top}$)}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt2\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(vaza)(uaza)(ubvb)})}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{z}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(vbzb)(uaQmva)(zaQmua)})}}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{u}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{u}_{a}\mathbf{z}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ub)^2(ubvb)(zaQmua)(zaQmva)})}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(\left(1+\tfrac{1}{m}\right)m\left(p-m+2\right)\left(2mp+4m-p^{2}-3p+2\right)\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(2\left(m^{2}p^{2}+5m^{2}p+2m^{2}-mp^{3}-7mp^{2}-16mp-12m+4p^{2}+16p+16\right)\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(2m^{2}p+4m^{2}-3mp^{2}-11mp+2m-p^{2}+5p+18\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Finally, the overall expression holds,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{6\left(m^{3}+m^{2}+2m+8\right)+4\left(m+2\right)p^{3}-2\left(5m^{2}+8m-2\right)p^{2}+\left(m-2\right)\left(5m^{2}+3m-16\right)p}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(p-m+2\right)\left(2m^{2}p+4m^{2}-3mp^{2}-11mp+2m-p^{2}+5p+18\right)}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{2m^{4}p+4m^{4}-7m^{3}p^{2}-18m^{3}p+8m^{2}p^{3}+25m^{2}p^{2}+24m^{2}p}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{20m^{2}-3mp^{4}-11mp^{3}-44mp^{2}-64mp-24m-p^{4}+11p^{3}+32p^{2}+68p+48}{\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 25
.

For
p\geq 4,m\in\{2,3,\dots,p\}
and a random transformation
\mathbf{O}
sampled as described in Eq. (
2.1
),
it holds that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{4}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{4}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-5m^{4}p-6m^{4}+18m^{3}p^{2}+34m^{3}p-12m^{3}-20m^{2}p^{3}-46m^{2}p^{2}-39m^{2}p-42m^{2}+6mp^{4}+10mp^{3}+96mp^{2}}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{154mp+60m+2p^{4}-30p^{3}-32p^{2}-144p-144}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Proof.

We begin by decomposing the expression into four terms,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{p}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\right)\left(\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{p}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{O}\mathbf{e}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

Below we compute each of these terms separately. The result in the proposition is given by summing these 4 terms.

Term 1.

Employing
12
once again, the term decomposes as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmza)(uaQmva)(xbzb)(xbvb)})}}\,.

The polynomial in the left inner term (the first summand) can be derived tediously, very much like in the proof of Prop.
23
,
and shown to hold

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{3}\left(2p^{3}+11p^{2}+p-30\right)+m^{2}\left(p^{4}+p^{3}+2p^{2}+60p+72\right)+2m\left(p^{4}+p^{3}+18p^{2}+14p-60\right)-8\left(2p+3\right)\left(p^{2}+12\right)}{\left(m+2\right)\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

Overall, the first term is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{-m^{3}\left(2p^{3}+11p^{2}+p-30\right)+m^{2}\left(p^{4}+p^{3}+2p^{2}+60p+72\right)+2m\left(p^{4}+p^{3}+18p^{2}+14p-60\right)-8\left(2p+3\right)\left(p^{2}+12\right)}{\left(m+2\right)\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-8m^{2}p+mp^{3}+4mp^{2}+15mp+18m-6p^{2}-6p-12\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{2m^{3}p^{2}+8m^{3}p-5m^{2}p^{3}-23m^{2}p^{2}-16m^{2}p+12m^{2}+2mp^{4}+9mp^{3}+45mp^{2}+86mp+24m-14p^{3}-18p^{2}-108p-144}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}.

Term 2.

Notice that
\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v},\mathbf{z},\mathbf{x}
are exchangeable in the sense that we can swap them freely (see Prop.
9
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Cor.~\ref {cor:odd-Q_m}}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{swap}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{swapped $\mathbf{x},\mathbf{u}$; solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(uaQmza)(uaQmva)(xbzb)(xbvb)})}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{b}\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right]}_{\text{swapped $\mathbf{v},\mathbf{u}$; solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ubvb)(xbzb)(ubxb)(vbzb)})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-8m^{2}p+mp^{3}+4mp^{2}+15mp+18m-6p^{2}-6p-12\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}+

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\frac{m\left(p-m\right)\left(5m^{2}p+6m^{2}-5mp^{2}-6mp+p^{3}+p^{2}+2p\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(p-m\right)\left(\tfrac{5m^{3}p+6m^{3}-7m^{2}p^{2}-14m^{2}p+2mp^{3}+5mp^{2}+17mp+18m-6p^{2}-6p-12}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\right)\,.

Terms 3 and 4.

First, we notice that subterms 3 and 4 are equivalent (we can swap
\mathbf{z},\mathbf{v}
due to the invariance; Prop.
9
), that is

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{below}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-3m^{2}p+6m^{2}+mp^{3}-mp^{2}+9mp+18m+p^{3}-5p^{2}-4p-12\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,,

and so we focus on just one of them.

By employing Cor.
12
, we see that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\left(\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}+\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(\left(5m^{2}p+6m^{2}-5mp^{2}-6mp+p^{3}+p^{2}+2p\right)+\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-8m^{2}p+mp^{3}+4mp^{2}+15mp+18m-6p^{2}-6p-12\right)\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-3m^{2}p+6m^{2}+mp^{3}-mp^{2}+9mp+18m+p^{3}-5p^{2}-4p-12\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,,

where we used the following two derivations,
i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\cdot\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{v}_{a}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}u_{i}q_{ij}z_{j}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}x_{k}q_{k\ell}v_{\ell}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\cdot u_{i}v_{\ell}x_{k}z_{j}q_{ij}q_{k\ell}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j,k,\ell=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\cdot u_{i}v_{\ell}x_{k}z_{j}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\left[q_{ij}q_{k\ell}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\left[\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Prop.~\ref {prop:odd}}\end{subarray}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{Q}_{m}}\left[q_{ij}^{2}\right]}_{=1/m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right)\cdot u_{i}v_{j}x_{i}z_{j}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{b}\cdot\mathbf{v}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(ubvb)(xbzb)(ubxb)(vbzb)})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(5m^{2}p+6m^{2}-5mp^{2}-6mp+p^{3}+p^{2}+2p\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{m}\mathbf{z}_{a}\cdot\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{z}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{r}\sim\mathcal{S}^{m-1}}\left(\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{r}\mathbf{r}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{a}\right)\cdot\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]=\frac{1}{m}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{z}_{a}\right\|^{2}\mathbf{u}_{a}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{a}\mathbf{u}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\mathbf{x}_{b}^{\top}\mathbf{v}_{b}\right]}_{\text{solved in Eq.~(\ref {eq:(za)^2(uaxa)(ubvb)(xbvb)})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(p-m\right)\left(-2m^{2}p^{2}-8m^{2}p+mp^{3}+4mp^{2}+15mp+18m-6p^{2}-6p-12\right)}{\left(p-3\right)\left(p-2\right)\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+1\right)\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\,.

∎

Proposition 26
.

Let
{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}
and let

{\mathbf{u}}_{a}
consists of its first
m
coordinates.
The expected
n
th
power of the squared norm is,

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{2n}=\prod_{r=0}^{n-1}\frac{m+2r}{p+2r}\,.

Specifically, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{2}&=\frac{m}{p}\\
&\\
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{4}&=\frac{m}{p}\cdot\frac{m+2}{p+2}\\
&\\
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{6}&=\frac{m}{p}\cdot\frac{m+2}{p+2}\cdot\frac{m+4}{p+4}\\
&\\
\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{8}&=\frac{m}{p}\cdot\frac{m+2}{p+2}\cdot\frac{m+4}{p+4}\cdot\frac{m+6}{p+6}\\
\Longrightarrow\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{b}}\right\|^{8}&=\frac{p-m}{p}\cdot\frac{p-m+2}{p+2}\cdot\frac{p-m+4}{p+4}\cdot\frac{p-m+6}{p+6}\end{split}

Proof.

Notice that the squared norm can be parameterized as

\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{2}={\mathbf{u}}_{a}^{\top}{\mathbf{u}}_{a}\triangleq\frac{X}{X+Y}\,,

where
X\sim\chi_{m}^{2},Y\sim\chi_{p-m}^{2}
.
Consequently, it is distributed as
\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{2}\sim B\left(\frac{m}{2},\frac{p-m}{2}\right)
.
Moreover, given
n\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, the
n
th
raw moment is given by
(Chapter 25 in
Johnson et al. 1995
),

\mathbb{E}_{{\mathbf{u}}\sim\mathcal{S}^{p-1}}\left\|{{\mathbf{u}}_{a}}\right\|^{2n}=\prod_{r=0}^{n-1}\frac{\frac{m}{2}+r}{\frac{p}{2}+r}=\prod_{r=0}^{n-1}\frac{m+2r}{p+2r}\,.

∎

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}u_{1}^{2}v_{1}^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}u_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{m}u_{j}^{2}\right)u_{1}^{2}v_{1}^{2}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}^{2}u_{j}^{2}u_{1}^{2}v_{1}^{2}\right]\\
&=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{6}v_{1}^{2}\right]}_{i=j=1}\!+2\left(m\!-\!1\right)\!\!\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{4}v_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{2}\right]}_{i=1\neq j\,\vee\,i\neq 1=j}+\left(m\!-\!1\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{4}v_{1}^{2}\right]}_{i=j\geq 2}+\left(m\!-\!1\right)\left(m\!-\!2\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{2}u_{3}^{2}v_{1}^{2}\right]}_{i\neq j\geq 2}\\
&=\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}6&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left(m-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4&2\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m-1\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&2\\
4&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m-1\right)\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&2\\
2&0\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\\
&=\frac{15\left(p-1\right)+\left(m-1\right)\left(6\left(p+1\right)+3\left(p+3\right)+\left(m-2\right)\left(p+3\right)\right)}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\\
&=\frac{m^{2}\left(p+3\right)+m\left(6p+6\right)+8p-24}{\left(p-1\right)p\left(p+2\right)\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)}\end{split}

(12)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbf{u}_{a}\right\|^{4}u_{1}^{2}v_{2}^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}u_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{m}u_{j}^{2}\right)u_{1}^{2}v_{2}^{2}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[u_{i}^{2}u_{j}^{2}u_{1}^{2}v_{2}^{2}\right]\\
&=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{6}v_{2}^{2}\right]}_{i=j=1}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{4}v_{2}^{2}\right]}_{i=j=2}+2\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{4}u_{2}^{2}v_{2}^{2}\right]}_{i=2,j=1\,\vee\,i=1,j=2}+2\left(m-2\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{4}v_{2}^{2}u_{3}^{2}\right]}_{i=1,j\geq 3\,\vee\,j=1,i\geq 3}+\\
&\hskip 10.00002pt2\left(m-2\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}u_{2}^{2}v_{2}^{2}u_{3}^{2}\right]}_{i=2,j\geq 3\,\vee\,j=2,i\geq 3}+\left(m-2\right)\left(m-3\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}v_{2}^{2}u_{3}^{2}u_{4}^{2}\right]}_{i\neq j\geq 3}+\left(m-2\right)\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}v_{2}^{2}u_{3}^{4}\right]}_{i=j\geq 3}\\
&=\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}6&0\\
0&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
4&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4&0\\
2&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4&0\\
0&2\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
2&2\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\\
&\hskip 10.00002pt\left(m-2\right)\left(m-3\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
0&2\\
2&0\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
0&2\\
4&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}6&0\\
0&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
4&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4&0\\
2&2\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+3\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}4&0\\
0&2\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+2\left(m-2\right)\left\langle\begin{smallmatrix}2&0\\
2&2\\
2&0\\
\overrightarrow{0}&\overrightarrow{0}\end{smallmatrix}\right\rangle+\\
